\displaystyle\frac{Ae^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\psi(e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha}))}{1+v^{b}}\chi_{1}(y,v,t)\leq\frac{T_{1}}{2},

where
\chi_{1}
is as in (
2.14
). On the other hand, if
y\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}
then it holds that
e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\geq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}
and thus

\displaystyle Ae^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\psi(e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha}))\chi_{2}(y,v,t)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A}{e^{\frac{t(m-1)}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|^{m}}\chi_{2}(y,v,t)\leq\frac{AC_{0}^{m-1}\epsilon}{|y-v^{\alpha}|}\chi_{2}(y,v,t)\leq\frac{T_{2}}{2},

where
\chi_{2}
is as in (
2.15
). We now focus on (
3.8
). By assumption it holds that
H_{n}\leq T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}
, where
T_{1},T_{2},T_{3}
are as in (
2.11
)-(
2.13
). We also have that
H_{\epsilon,\textup{in}}(y,v)\leq T_{1}(y,v,0)+T_{2}(y,v,0)+T_{3}(y,v,0)
. By Proposition
3.1
and Remark
3.2
, this implies that
H_{n+1}(y,v,z)\leq 4[T_{1}(y,v,z)+T_{2}(y,v,z)+T_{3}(y,v,z)]
, for all
z\in[0,\operatorname{min}\{\frac{\epsilon}{m},1,\tilde{\delta}\}]
, with
\tilde{\delta}
as in Proposition
3.1
. Thus, for all
t\in[0,\operatorname{min}\{\frac{\epsilon}{m},1,\tilde{\delta}\}]
, it holds that

\displaystyle H_{n+1}(y,v,t)

\displaystyle\leq S_{\epsilon}(t)[H_{n+1}(y,v,0)]+2K_{0}v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{(0,\frac{v}{2})}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[H_{n+1}(y,v-v^{\prime},s)H_{n}(y,v^{\prime},s)\bigg]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\frac{T_{1}+T_{2}}{2}+8K_{0}v^{\gamma}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{(0,\frac{v}{2})}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[T_{i}(y,v-v^{\prime},s)T_{j}(y,v^{\prime},s)\bigg]\textup{d}s.

We will prove in Proposition
3.6
that for
m>\frac{2(\gamma+1)}{\alpha}
and
b\geq\gamma+1
, there exist a time
T(A)
, which depends on
A
, and
\epsilon_{1}\in(0,1)
, which depends on
A
, such that

\displaystyle 8K_{0}v^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{(0,\frac{v}{2})}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[T_{i}(y,v-v^{\prime},s)T_{j}(y,v^{\prime},s)\bigg]\textup{d}s\leq\frac{T_{1}+T_{2}}{2},

(3.9)

\displaystyle\textup{ for all }y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha},t\leq\frac{1}{T(A)},\epsilon\leq\epsilon_{1}

and thus

\displaystyle H_{n+1}(y,v,t)\leq T_{1}+T_{2},\textup{ for all }t\in[0,\operatorname{min}\{\epsilon,1,\tilde{\delta}\}],\quad y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}.

From Section
4
, we also have that if
b\geq\overline{b}(\gamma,\alpha)
,
m\geq\frac{b}{\alpha}+1
it holds that

\displaystyle H_{n+1}(y,v,t)\leq T_{3},\quad y\leq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}.

Thus
H_{n+1}(y,v,t)\leq T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}
at
t:=\operatorname{min}\{\epsilon,1,\tilde{\delta}\}
and we can then use Proposition
3.1
and iterate the argument in order to extend the result for all times
t\leq\frac{1}{T(A)}
, where
T(A)
is as in (
3.9
).
∎

Remark 3.4
.

Here and in all the following, we only treat the case when
(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}
since the case
(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\geq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}
follows similarly and with fewer technicalities. On the one hand, if
(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\geq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}
, then
\{y\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\}\subseteq\{y\leq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}
and thus we can bound the region
\{y\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\}
as in Section
4
. On the other hand, we can bound the region
\{y\geq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\}
using the estimates from Proposition
3.9
and then we replace
\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}}
in the proof of Proposition
3.6
by
\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\}}
. Notice we do not use Proposition
3.10
in this case.

Remark 3.5
.

Notice that in order to prove (
3.9
), since we are estimating the function on the set
\{y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}
, we actually only need to bound

\displaystyle S_{\epsilon}(t)H_{\epsilon,\textup{in}}(y,v)+\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}}v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{i}(y,v-w,s)T_{j}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s.

We now focus on proving (
3.9
). More precisely, we prove that

Proposition 3.6
.

Let
\epsilon\in(0,1)
and
S_{\epsilon}
as in (
2.7
). Let
m>\frac{2(\gamma+1)}{\alpha}
and
b\geq\gamma+1
. There exist a time
T(A)\in[0,1]
,
\epsilon_{1}\in(0,1)
, and
C_{0}
, which are dependent on
A
as in (
2.1
) and on the parameters
\alpha,\gamma,b,m
, such that if
t\leq T(A)
,
\epsilon\leq\epsilon_{1}
, then the following estimate holds for all
y\in\mathbb{R}
and
v\in(0,\infty)
.

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}}8K_{0}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{i}(y,v-w,s)T_{j}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s\leq\frac{T_{1}(y,v,t)+T_{2}(y,v,t)}{2}.

(3.10)

Remark 3.7
.

We remember that
T_{2}
as in (
2.12
) depends on
C_{0}
and this is the reason why we require
C_{0}
to depend on
A
in the statement of Proposition
3.6
in order for (
3.10
) to hold. We refer to (
2.9
) for the exact choice of
C_{0}
and to Proposition
3.10
and to (
3.21
) in order to see why this definition was required.

We first prove upper bounds for
\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{i}(y,v-w,s)T_{j}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w
and then make use of Lemma
2.16
. However, despite the fact that we only need to analyze the region
{\{y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}}
as noticed in Remark
3.5
, when proving the inequalities before applying the semigroup, we need to analyze a wider range of values of
y
. More precisely, we have the following.

Lemma 3.8
.

Let
\epsilon\in(0,1)
,
t\in[0,1],
and
s\in[0,\frac{t}{2}]
. Let
S_{\epsilon}
as in (
2.7
),
y\in\mathbb{R},
and
v\in(0,\infty)
. There exists
\overline{\epsilon}\in(0,1)
sufficiently small such that for all
\epsilon\leq\overline{\epsilon}
, there exists
X(\epsilon)\leq 0
such that
S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\big[\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq X(\epsilon)\}}\big]=\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}}
.

Proof.

It holds that

\displaystyle S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\big[\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq X(\epsilon)\}}\big]=\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}}

\displaystyle\textup{ if and only if }\mathbbm{1}_{\{e^{\frac{t-s}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha})+v^{\alpha}\geq X(\epsilon)\}}=\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}}.

We then have that
e^{-\frac{t-s}{\epsilon}}(X(\epsilon)-v^{\alpha})+v^{\alpha}=\Big(\frac{v}{3}\Big)^{\alpha}
if and only if
X(\epsilon)=e^{\frac{t-s}{\epsilon}}\Big(\Big(\frac{v}{3}\Big)^{\alpha}-v^{\alpha}\Big)+v^{\alpha}=v^{\alpha}\bigg(1-e^{\frac{t-s}{\epsilon}}\bigg)+e^{\frac{t-s}{\epsilon}}\Big(\frac{v}{3}\Big)^{\alpha}.
In other words, for sufficiently small
\epsilon
we can have that
X(\epsilon)\leq 0
.
∎

We have the following estimates. We first prove estimates for the convolution of
T_{1},T_{3}
with themselves considering different regions of
y
.

Proposition 3.9

(Estimates for
T_{1}
and
T_{3}
)
.

Let
\epsilon\in(0,1)
,
t\in[0,1],
and
s\in[0,t]
. Let
S_{\epsilon}
as in (
2.7
). Let
i,j\in\{1,3\}
. Let
b\geq\gamma+1-\alpha
and
m>\frac{2(\gamma+1)}{\alpha}
. Let
T_{1}
and
T_{3}
be as in (
2.11
) and (
2.13
). There exists a constant
C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)>0
, which depends on
\alpha,\gamma,b,m
, such that for all
y\in\mathbb{R}
and
v\in(0,\infty)
the following estimates hold.

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{i}(y,v-w,s)T_{j}(y,w,s)

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\textup{d}w\bigg]\textup{d}s\leq\frac{C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{2}te^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}}{(1+e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}};

(3.11)

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{i}(y,v-w,s)T_{j}(y,w,s)

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq v^{\alpha}\}}\textup{d}w\bigg]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}^{2}}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{2}\epsilon\operatorname{min}\{1,e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\}}{|y-v^{\alpha}|}.

(3.12)

We then focus on proving estimates for the remaining possible combinations of the convolution of
T_{i}
with
T_{j}
.

Proposition 3.10

(Estimates for the rest of combinations)
.

Let
\epsilon\in(0,1)
,
t\in[0,1],
and
s\in[0,t]
. Let
S_{\epsilon}
as in (
2.7
). Let
i\in\{2,3\}
. Let
b\geq\gamma+1
and
m>\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}+2
. Let
T_{1},\ldots,T_{3}
as in (
2.11
)-(
2.13
). There exists a constant
C_{2}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)>0
, which depends on
\alpha,\gamma,b,m
, such that for all
y\in\mathbb{R}
and
v\in(0,\infty)
the following estimates hold.

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{i-1}(y,v-w,s)T_{i}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s=0;

(3.13)

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{i}(y,v-w,s)T_{i-1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}\epsilon[t+\epsilon+C_{0}^{-(m-1)}]}{v^{\alpha}-y}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq v^{\alpha}-e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}C^{-1}_{0}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}};

(3.14)

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{2}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{4}C_{0}^{2(m-1)}\epsilon(t+\epsilon)^{2}}{v^{\alpha}-y}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq v^{\alpha}-e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}C^{-1}_{0}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}.

(3.15)

Assuming the estimates in Proposition
3.9
and Proposition
3.10
hold true, we are now able to prove Proposition
3.6
.

Proof of Proposition
3.6
.

We wish to prove (
3.10
). We remember that
C_{0}^{m-1}=M_{1}A
, see (
2.9
). More precisely,
C_{0}
is of order
A^{\frac{1}{m-1}}
. We choose
M_{1}
, depending on
M_{2}
, such that

\displaystyle 8K_{0}\operatorname{max}\{C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m),C_{2}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)\}M_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{M_{1}}{24},

(3.16)

where
C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)
is as in Proposition
3.9
and
C_{2}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)
is as in Proposition
3.10
.

From (
3.11
), we can choose a sufficiently small
T
, which depends on
A
, such that for all
t\leq T
, it holds that

\displaystyle 8K_{0}\sum_{i,j\in\{1,3\}}v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}

\displaystyle T_{i}(y,v-w,s)T_{j}(y,w,s)\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\textup{d}w\bigg]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq 8K_{0}C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)AtT_{1}(y,v,t)\leq\frac{T_{1}(y,v,t)}{12}.

We now estimate (
3.12
). We prove in this case that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}}\frac{8K_{0}C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}^{2}}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{2}\epsilon\operatorname{min}\{1,e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\}}{|y-v^{\alpha}|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{T_{1}(y,v,t)}{12}+\frac{T_{2}(y,v,t)}{12}.

(3.17)

•

Let us assume first that
e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\leq 1
. Then

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon}{|y-v^{\alpha}|(1+v^{b})}\operatorname{min}\{1,e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\}=\frac{\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{(1+v^{b})}\leq\frac{2\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{mt}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|^{m})(1+v^{b})}.

(3.18)

Thus, if
e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\leq 1
, then for sufficiently small
\epsilon
, which depends on
A
, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{8K_{0}C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}^{2}}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{2}\epsilon\operatorname{min}\{1,e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\}}{|y-v^{\alpha}|}

\displaystyle\leq 8K_{0}C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}^{2}A\epsilon T_{1}(y,v,t)\leq\frac{T_{1}(y,v,t)}{24}.

(3.19)

•

If
C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\geq e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\geq 1
, then

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon}{|y-v^{\alpha}|}\operatorname{min}\{1,e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\}\mathbbm{1}_{\{1\leq e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\leq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}=\frac{\epsilon}{|y-v^{\alpha}|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{1\leq e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\leq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{1\leq e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\leq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}\leq\frac{C_{0}^{-(m-1)}e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{(e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{1\leq e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\leq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2C_{0}^{-(m-1)}e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{1+(e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{1\leq e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\leq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}.

(3.20)

It thus follows by (
3.16
) that

\displaystyle\frac{8K_{0}C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}^{2}}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{2}\epsilon\operatorname{min}\{1,e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\}}{|y-v^{\alpha}|}

\displaystyle\leq 8K_{0}C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}^{2}AC_{0}^{-(m-1)}T_{1}(y,v,t)\leq\frac{T_{1}(y,v,t)}{24}.

(3.21)

•

If
e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\geq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}
, then by the choice of
T_{2}
in (
2.12
)

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{v^{\alpha}\geq y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}}\frac{8K_{0}C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}^{2}}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{2}\epsilon}{|y-v^{\alpha}|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\geq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}

\displaystyle\leq 8K_{0}C_{1}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}^{2}C_{0}^{-(m-1)}T_{2}(y,v,t)\leq\frac{T_{2}(y,v,t)}{12}.

(3.22)

Combining estimates (
3.33
)-(
3.2
) together with the fact that
S_{\epsilon}(t-s)(\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq v^{\alpha}\}})=\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq v^{\alpha}\}}
which follows by Item
1
of Lemma

2.16
, we obtain (
3.17
).

We now proceed with (
3.14
). If
t,\epsilon
are sufficiently small and for sufficiently large
C_{0}
, then

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}}8K_{0}v^{\gamma}\sum_{i\in\{2,3\}}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{i}(y,v-w,s)T_{i-1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8K_{0}C_{2}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}\epsilon[t+\epsilon+C_{0}^{-(m-1)}]}{v^{\alpha}-y}\chi_{2}(y,v,t)\leq\frac{T_{2}(y,v,t)}{24}.

This is since by (
3.16
), it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{8K_{0}C_{2}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}C_{0}^{-(m-1)}A}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{2}C_{0}^{m-1}\epsilon}{v^{\alpha}-y}\chi_{2}(y,v,t)

\displaystyle=4K_{0}C_{2}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)M_{2}C_{0}^{-(m-1)}AT_{2}(y,v,t)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{T_{2}(y,v,t)}{48}.

(3.23)

Similarly, for (
3.15
), there exist
T
and
\epsilon_{1}
, which depend on
A
, such that for all
t\leq T
and
\epsilon\leq\epsilon_{1}
, then

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}\}}8K_{0}v^{\gamma}\sum_{i\in\{2,3\}}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{2}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8K_{0}C_{2}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{4}C_{0}^{2(m-1)}\epsilon(t+\epsilon)^{2}}{v^{\alpha}-y}\chi_{2}(y,v,t)

\displaystyle\leq 8K_{0}C_{2}(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)A^{2}C_{0}^{m-1}(t+\epsilon)^{2}T_{2}(y,v,t)\leq\frac{T_{2}(y,v,t)}{24}.

This concludes our proof.
∎

The rest of this section is dedicated to proving Proposition
3.9
and Proposition
3.10
.

3.3
Proof of Proposition
3.9

Proof of Proposition

3.9
.

Let
i,j\in\{1,3\}
. We focus on finding an upper bound for

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{i}(y,v-w,s)T_{j}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s.

(3.24)

We notice that since
w\in[0,\frac{v}{2}]
, it holds that

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{i}(y,v-w,s)T_{j}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{C(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)A^{2}v^{\gamma}}{(1+w^{b})(1+(v-w)^{b})}\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha}))\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-(v-w)^{\alpha})\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)A^{2}v^{\gamma}}{1+v^{b}}\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{1}{1+w^{b}}\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha}))\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-(v-w)^{\alpha})\textup{d}w.

Since
w\in[0,\frac{v}{2}]
, it holds that
w^{\alpha}\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}
, for all
v>0,w\in[0,\frac{v}{2}]
. By (
2.43
), we have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha}))\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-(v-w)^{\alpha})\textup{d}w

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{C(m)v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{C(m)v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}=:I_{1}+I_{2}.

(3.25)

Proof of (
3.11
).
We will prove that there exists a constant
C(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)
, which depends on
\alpha
,
\gamma
,
b
,
m
, such that

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{1}(y,v-w,s)T_{1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\bigg]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4A^{2}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}}{(1+w^{b})(1+(v-w)^{b})}\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha}))\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-(v-w)^{\alpha})\textup{d}w\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\bigg]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)}{1+v^{b}}\frac{A^{2}te^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{tm}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|^{m}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}.

In order to do this, we will first prove some estimates for

\displaystyle e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{(1+w^{b})(1+(v-w)^{b})}\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha}))\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-(v-w)^{\alpha})\textup{d}w

(3.26)

and then use Item
1
of Lemma

2.16
to obtain a bound for (
3.24
). Let
\delta\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
be sufficiently small. We start by estimating
I_{1}
in (
3.25
). We analyze separately the regions
w\in[0,\delta v]
and
w\in[\delta v,\frac{v}{2}]
. Moreover, since we are in the case when
y\geq v^{\alpha}
, we have that
y-w^{\alpha}\geq y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}\geq C(\alpha)v^{\alpha}
and
y-w^{\alpha}\geq y-v^{\alpha}\geq 0
. We analyze first
w\in[\delta v,\frac{v}{2}]
. In this case

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{C(\delta,b)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+v^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}}{1+v^{b}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}},

(3.27)

where in the last line we used Lemma
2.14
for
\beta=m
and then the fact that
b\geq\gamma+1-\alpha
. Notice also that since
\gamma>1
and
\alpha\in(0,1)
, we have that
\gamma+1-\alpha>0
.

We now analyze the region where
w\leq\delta v
. Then
(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}>(1-\delta)^{\alpha}v^{\alpha}-\delta^{\alpha}v^{\alpha}\geq C(\delta,\alpha)v^{\alpha}
since
\delta<\frac{1}{2}
. Then, since
m>\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m+1-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}}{1+v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}},

(3.28)

where in the last line we used Lemma
2.14
for
\beta=m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}+1
.

We now estimate
I_{2}
in (
3.25
). We start by analyzing the region
w\in[\delta v,\frac{v}{2}]
. Following the estimates for
I_{1}
, when
y\geq v^{\alpha}
it holds that
y-(v-w)^{\alpha}\geq y-v^{\alpha}\geq 0
. By Lemma
2.14
, we thus have as before that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}}.

In the region where
w\in[0,\delta v]
, we have that
(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}\geq C(\delta)v^{\alpha}
and thus we obtain as before that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}}.

Combining all the above estimates and since
b>2
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{(1+w^{b})(1+(v-w)^{b})}\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha}))\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-(v-w)^{\alpha})\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,b,m)}{1+v^{b}}

and then using Item
1
of Lemma

2.16
it follows that when
y\geq v^{\alpha},
it holds that

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{1}(y,v-w,s)T_{1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\bigg]\textup{d}s\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,b,m)te^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{A^{2}}{1+v^{b}}.

Proof of (
3.12
).

•

Case
y\leq 0
:
We will prove estimates for (
3.26
) in this case too.

We have that
0\geq 2^{-\alpha}(y-v^{\alpha})\geq y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}
and thus
|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|\geq 2^{-\alpha}|y-v^{\alpha}|
. Moreover, for
w\in[0,\frac{v}{2}]
, it holds that

\displaystyle y-(v-w)^{\alpha}\leq y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}\leq-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}\leq 0.

(3.29)

We thus have that

\displaystyle|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|\geq|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|\geq 2^{-\alpha}|y-v^{\alpha}|\textup{ and }|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|\geq C(\alpha)v^{-\alpha}.

(3.30)

As in (
3.25
), by (
2.43
), it holds that

\displaystyle e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha}))\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-(v-w)^{\alpha})\textup{d}w\leq I_{1}+I_{2},

with
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
as in (
3.25
). We start by estimating
I_{2}
in (
3.25
). We first analyze the region where
w\in[\delta v,\frac{v}{2}]
. By (
3.30
), we have that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq 0\}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq 0\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m-1}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{v^{\gamma-\alpha}}{1+v^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

(3.31)

and we use Lemma
2.14
in order to conclude that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq 0\}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq 0\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)}{(1+|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m-1}}\frac{v^{\gamma+1-2\alpha}}{1+v^{b}}

(3.32)

in this case.

If
w\in[0,\delta v]
, by (
3.30
) and (
3.3
), it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq 0\}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq 0\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma)}{(1+|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m-1}}.

We now estimate
I_{1}
in (
3.25
). We analyze the region
w\in[\delta v,\frac{v}{2}]
. In this region, it holds that
y-w^{\alpha}\leq y-\delta^{\alpha}v^{\alpha}\leq-\delta^{\alpha}v^{\alpha}\leq 0
and
y-w^{\alpha}\leq y-\delta^{\alpha}v^{\alpha}\leq\delta^{\alpha}(y-v^{\alpha})\leq 0
. As such, we can proceed as in (
3.31
) and (
3.32
) in order to prove that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq 0\}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq 0\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)}{(1+|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m-1}}.

We now estimate
I_{1}
in (
3.25
) in the region
w\in[0,\delta v]
. We have that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq 0\}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\in[-v^{\alpha},0]\}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle+\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq-v^{\alpha}\}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}.

For the region
y\leq-v^{\alpha}
we have that

\displaystyle y-w^{\alpha}\leq y=\frac{y}{2}+\frac{y}{2}\leq\frac{y-v^{\alpha}}{2}\leq 0\textup{ and }y-w^{\alpha}\leq-v^{\alpha}\leq 0.

Thus, when
y\leq-v^{\alpha}
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq-v^{\alpha}\}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq-v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{C(m)v^{\gamma-\alpha}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m-1}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

and we can proceed as in (
3.3
) in order to show that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq-v^{\alpha}\}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,m)}{(1+|y-v^{\alpha}|)^{m-1}}.

On the other hand, if
y\in[-v^{\alpha},0]
and
w\in[0,\delta v]
, we have that
v^{\alpha}\leq|y-v^{\alpha}|\leq 2v^{\alpha}
and that
|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|\geq C(\delta,\alpha)v^{\alpha}
. Thus

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\in[-v^{\alpha},0]\}}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\in[-v^{\alpha},0]\}}v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\in[-v^{\alpha},0]\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,m)v^{\gamma}}{1+|y-v^{\alpha}|^{\frac{m}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{\frac{m}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\in[-v^{\alpha},0]\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,m)v^{\gamma-\alpha}}{1+|y-v^{\alpha}|^{\frac{m}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{\frac{m}{2}-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\in[-v^{\alpha},0]\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,m)}{1+|y-v^{\alpha}|^{\frac{m}{2}}}\frac{v^{\gamma-\alpha}}{1+v^{\gamma+1-2\alpha}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{\frac{m}{2}+1-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\in[-v^{\alpha},0]\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,m)}{1+|y-v^{\alpha}|^{\frac{m}{2}}},

where in the last line we used Lemma
2.14
for
\beta=\frac{m}{2}+1-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}
.

Combining all the above estimates and performing similar computations as the ones in Proposition
2.15
and since
m>\frac{2(\gamma+1)}{\alpha}>2
, we obtain that

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{1}(y,w,s)T_{1}(y,v-w,s)\textup{d}w\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq 0\}}\bigg]\textup{d}s\leq\frac{C(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)A^{2}\epsilon\operatorname{min}\{1,e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\}}{(1+v^{b})|y-v^{\alpha}|}.

(3.33)

•

Case
y\in[0,v^{\alpha}]
:
We have in this case that
|y-v^{\alpha}|\leq v^{\alpha}
.

•

Subcase
v\geq 1
:
We will prove that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{0\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}\}}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}}{(1+w^{b})(1+(v-w)^{b})}\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha}))\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-(v-w)^{\alpha})\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+v^{b})(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{l}},

(3.34)

for
l=\frac{m}{2}
. Notice that
l>2
since
m>\frac{2(\gamma+1)}{\alpha}>4.
If (
3.3
) holds true, then we prove that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{sd}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{l}}\bigg]\textup{d}s\leq\frac{C(\alpha)\epsilon\operatorname{min}\{1,e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\}}{|y-v^{\alpha}|}.

(3.35)

We now prove (
3.35
). Assume first that
|y-v^{\alpha}|\geq e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}
. It follows that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{sl}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{l}}\bigg]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}s}{1+e^{\frac{sl}{\epsilon}}|(y-v^{\alpha})e^{\frac{t-s}{\epsilon}}+v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|^{l}}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}s}{1+e^{\frac{tl}{\epsilon}}|(y-v^{\alpha})+e^{-\frac{t-s}{\epsilon}}\big(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}\big)|^{l}}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}s}{1+e^{\frac{tl}{\epsilon}}|(y-v^{\alpha})+e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\big(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}\big)|^{l}},

where in the last line we made the change of variables
z=t-s
. Making now the change of variable
z=\frac{s}{\epsilon}
, we further obtain that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{sl}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{l}}\bigg]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle=\epsilon\int_{0}^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}s}{1+e^{\frac{tl}{\epsilon}}|(y-v^{\alpha})+e^{-s}\big(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}\big)|^{l}}.

We further make the change of variables
z=y-v^{\alpha}+e^{-s}\big(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}\big)
. Notice that

\displaystyle\textup{d}z

\displaystyle=-e^{-s}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})\textup{d}s;

\displaystyle e^{-s}

\displaystyle=\frac{z+(v^{\alpha}-y)}{v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}};

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\textup{d}s}{\textup{d}z}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{z+(v^{\alpha}-y)}.

It then follows that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{sl}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{l}}\bigg]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle=\epsilon\int_{y-v^{\alpha}+e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}^{y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}z}{1+e^{\frac{tl}{\epsilon}}|z|^{l}}\bigg|\frac{\textup{d}s}{\textup{d}z}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\epsilon\int_{y-v^{\alpha}+e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}^{y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}z}{1+e^{\frac{tl}{\epsilon}}|z|^{l}}\frac{1}{z+(v^{\alpha}-y)}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{sl}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{l}}\bigg]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle=\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\int_{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha})+(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z|^{l}}\frac{1}{z+e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)}

\displaystyle=\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{l}}\frac{1}{z}.

We thus have that

\displaystyle\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{l}}\frac{1}{z}

\displaystyle=\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\leq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{l}}\frac{1}{z}

\displaystyle+\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\geq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{l}}\frac{1}{z}.

For the first term, since
v\geq 1
, we have that

\displaystyle\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\leq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{l}}\frac{1}{z}\leq\frac{C(\alpha)\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{v^{\alpha}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\leq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{l}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\alpha)\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\leq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{l-1}}\leq\frac{C(\alpha)\epsilon}{(v^{\alpha}-y)},

since
l>2
, which is the correct estimate for the region
|y-v^{\alpha}|\geq e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}
.

For the second term, we have that

\displaystyle\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\geq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{l}}\frac{1}{z}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\epsilon}{v^{\alpha}-y}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\geq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{l}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\epsilon}{v^{\alpha}-y},

which concludes our proof in the case when
v\geq 1,|y-v^{\alpha}|\geq e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}
.

We now focus on the case
v\geq 1,|y-v^{\alpha}|\leq e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}
. Since
v\geq 1
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{sd}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{l}}\bigg]\textup{d}s\leq\frac{C(\alpha)\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{v^{\alpha}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{l}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\alpha)\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{l}}\leq C(\alpha)\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}.

We now focus on proving (
3.3
) when
v\geq 1
. We assume without loss of generality that
|y-w^{\alpha}|\geq|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|
since the region
|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|\geq|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|
can be treated similarly. As before, we analyze separately the regions
w\in[\delta v,\frac{v}{2}]
and
w\in(0,\delta v),
for some
\delta<\frac{1}{2}
. First, we notice that

\displaystyle\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}

\displaystyle\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}}{(1+w^{b})(1+(v-w)^{b})}\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha}))\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-(v-w)^{\alpha})\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\delta,b)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+v^{b})(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}},

where we used Lemma
2.14
.

For the region where
w\in[0,\delta v]
, we will prove that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,m)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{l}},

for some
l=\frac{m}{2}>2
as before and then (
3.3
) follows by (
3.25
). We can assume without loss of generality that
|y-w^{\alpha}|\geq|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|
.
Moreover, since when
w\in[0,\delta v]
it holds that
(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}\geq C(\delta,\alpha)v^{\alpha}
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}+1}}\frac{1}{1+v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}}{(1+v^{\gamma+1-\alpha})(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}},

where in the last line we used Lemma
2.14
.

We now estimate
I_{2}
.
When
w\in[0,\delta v]
, then
(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}\geq C(\delta,\alpha)v^{\alpha}\geq C(\delta,\alpha)|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|
and we have that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{0\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}\}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{v^{\gamma}}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{0\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}\}}C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,m)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|(v-w)^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}|)^{\frac{m}{2}-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}+1}}\frac{1}{1+v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}}\frac{1}{1+e^{\frac{sm}{2\epsilon}}v^{\frac{\alpha m}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{0\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,m)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{sm}{2\epsilon}}v^{\frac{\alpha m}{2}}}\leq\mathbbm{1}_{\{0\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}\}}\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,m)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{sm}{2\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|^{\frac{m}{2}}},

which concludes our proof.

•

Subcase
v\leq 1
:
We will prove that

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{0\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}\}}\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}}{(1+w^{b})(1+(v-w)^{b})}\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha}))\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-(v-w)^{\alpha})\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\alpha,b)v^{\gamma}e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+v^{b})(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}}.

(3.36)

If (
3.3
) holds true, then as in the case
v\geq 1
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}}{(1+e^{\frac{sm}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\bigg]\textup{d}s=\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{m}}\frac{1}{z}.

For the region
v\leq 1,|y-v^{\alpha}|\leq e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}
, we have that

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{(1+e^{\frac{sm}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\bigg]\textup{d}s\leq C(\alpha)\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma-\alpha}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\alpha)\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma-\alpha}\leq C(\alpha)\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}},

since
\gamma>1>\alpha
and
v\leq 1
.

For the region
|y-v^{\alpha}|\geq e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}
, it follows as before that

\displaystyle\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\leq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{v^{\gamma}\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{m}}\frac{1}{z}\leq C(\alpha)\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\leq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{v^{\gamma-\alpha}\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\alpha)\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma-\alpha}}{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\leq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{m-1}}\leq\frac{C(\alpha)\epsilon}{(v^{\alpha}-y)},

since
v\leq 1
. In the same manner, we have that

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\epsilon e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\geq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{m}}\frac{1}{z}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\epsilon v^{\gamma}}{v^{\alpha}-y}\int_{(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}),|z|\geq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z-e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)|^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\epsilon}{v^{\alpha}-y},

since
\gamma>1
and
v\leq 1
.
We now focus on proving (
3.3
) when
v\leq 1
. We assume without loss of generality that
|y-w^{\alpha}|\geq|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|
. It holds that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{2s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}}{(1+w^{b})(1+(v-w)^{b})}\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha}))\psi(e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(y-(v-w)^{\alpha})\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{C(b)}{1+v^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\textup{d}w}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|)^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{C(\alpha,b)v^{1-\alpha}}{1+v^{b}}\leq\frac{C(\alpha,b)e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}v^{\gamma}}{(1+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|)^{m}}\frac{1}{1+v^{b}},

where in the last line we used Lemma
2.14
and the fact that
\alpha<1
.

∎

3.4
Proof of Proposition

Proof of Proposition
3.10
.

Proof of (
3.13
).
Let
i\in\{2,3\}
. We want to bound from above the term

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{i-1}(y,v-w,s)T_{i}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s.

(3.37)

We start by estimating

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{1}(y,v-w,s)T_{2}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{4A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}}{(1+w^{b})(1+(v-w)^{b})}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|^{m}}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-w^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w=0.

Thus, this term does not play any role. Similarly

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{3}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{2M_{2}A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}}{(1+w^{b})(1+(v-w)^{b})}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq(\frac{v-w}{3})^{\alpha}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(\frac{w}{3})^{\alpha}\}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}\textup{d}w=0.

Proof of (
3.14
).

i)

We continue by estimating from above the term

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s.

(3.38)

In the following, we bound first

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s,

(3.39)

for some sufficiently small but fixed
\delta>0
. We focus then on bounding

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\delta v}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s.

We start by estimating

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}

\displaystyle T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C(\delta,b)A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C(\delta,b)A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}\frac{\epsilon}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\times\mathbbm{1}_{\{-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}.

Remark 3.11
.

Notice that the region
y\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}
implies that after the action of the semigroup we have that
e^{\frac{t-s}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha})+v^{\alpha}\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}
or in other words after the action of the semigroup we are in the region where
e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\geq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}
.

We can write
\mathbbm{1}_{\{-C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}
as a sum of the form

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{-C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}=\mathbbm{1}_{\{-C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq y\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}

\displaystyle+\mathbbm{1}_{\{(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq y\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}.

We analyze first the region where
\{-C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq y\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}
. In this region, since
|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|\geq|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|
, then

\displaystyle\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\leq\frac{\epsilon}{|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}.

Thus, it holds that

\displaystyle I_{1}(y,v,s):=v^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\mathbbm{1}_{\{-C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq y\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C(\delta,b)A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{\epsilon}{|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\times\mathbbm{1}_{\{-C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq y\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)\epsilon}{|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|}\frac{A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{-C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq y\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}},

where in the last line we used Lemma
2.14
.

Then

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[I_{1}(y,v,s)\bigg]\textup{d}s\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{e^{\frac{t-s}{\epsilon}}}{|e^{\frac{t-s}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha})+v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|}

\displaystyle\times\mathbbm{1}_{\{-C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq e^{\frac{t-s}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha})+v^{\alpha}\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}\textup{d}s

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{|e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha})+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})|}

\displaystyle\times\mathbbm{1}_{\{-e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha})\leq e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}((\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-v^{\alpha})-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}\textup{d}s

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{|e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})|}

\displaystyle\times\mathbbm{1}_{\{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}v^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\geq e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)\geq e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})+C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}\textup{d}s

\displaystyle=:C(\delta,\alpha,b)L(y,v,t).

(3.40)

We make the change of variables
z=e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})
. We thus have that

\displaystyle L(y,v,t)=

\displaystyle\frac{A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\int_{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}((\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-y)}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-z}\frac{1}{|z|}

\displaystyle\times\mathbbm{1}_{\{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\geq z\geq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}\textup{d}z.

(3.41)

On the one hand, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\int_{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}((\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-y),z\leq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)}{2}}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-z}\frac{1}{|z|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\geq z\geq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}\textup{d}z

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}\int_{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}((\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-y),z\leq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)}{2}}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{1}{|z|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\geq z\geq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}\textup{d}z

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}\int_{1}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{1}{z}\textup{d}z\leq\frac{C(\alpha)v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}\bigg[\frac{t}{\epsilon}+|\ln{v^{\alpha}}|\bigg],

(3.42)

where in (
3.42
) we used that we are in the region
e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}v^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\geq e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)\geq e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})+C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}
from (
3.40
) and that
C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\leq v^{\alpha}\big[1-3^{-\alpha}\big]
. We then have that

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}\bigg[\frac{t}{\epsilon}+|\ln{v^{\alpha}}|\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}t\epsilon}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}+\frac{v^{\gamma+1}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)};\qquad v\geq 1,

(3.43)

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}\bigg[\frac{t}{\epsilon}+|\ln{v^{\alpha}}|\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}t\epsilon}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}+\frac{v^{\gamma-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)};\qquad v\leq 1.

(3.44)

We then use that
b\geq\gamma+1
in (
3.43
).

On the other hand, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\int_{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}((\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-y),z\geq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)}{2}}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-z}\frac{1}{|z|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\geq z\geq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}\textup{d}z

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}\int_{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}((\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-y),z\geq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)}{2}}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{1}{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-z}\mathbbm{1}_{\{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\geq z\geq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}\textup{d}z

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}\int_{1}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})}\frac{1}{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-z}\textup{d}z

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}\int_{v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}}^{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-y)-1}\frac{1}{z}\textup{d}z\leq\frac{C(\alpha)v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}\epsilon^{2}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}(v^{\alpha}-y)}\bigg[\frac{t}{\epsilon}+|\ln{v^{\alpha}}|\bigg]

(3.45)

which concludes our proof.

We now estimate the region where
\{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|\leq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}
. In this region, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{\frac{m}{m-1}}}{2}\leq\frac{C_{0}^{-m}}{1+e^{\frac{sm}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|^{m}}.

Thus

\displaystyle\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}\frac{C_{0}^{-m}\epsilon^{-\frac{m}{m-1}}}{1+e^{\frac{sm}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|^{m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2C_{0}^{-(m-1)}e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{sm}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|^{m}}.

Denote by

\displaystyle I_{2}(y,v,s):=v^{\gamma}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\mathbbm{1}_{\{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|\leq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}.

Thus, in this region it holds that

\displaystyle I_{2}(y,v,s)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\delta,b)C_{0}^{m-1}A^{3}C_{0}^{-(m-1)}v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{sm}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|^{m}}\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)C_{0}^{m-1}A^{3}e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{sm}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|^{m}}\frac{C_{0}^{-(m-1)}v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}},

where in the last line we used Lemma
2.14
.

Thus, in this region, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\big[I_{2}(y,v,s)\big]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)C_{0}^{m-1}A^{3}C_{0}^{-(m-1)}v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{|e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha})+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})|\leq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}}{1+|e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha})+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})|^{m}}\textup{d}s.

We then use (
3.35
),
m>2
, in order to deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\big[I_{2}(y,v,s)\big]\textup{d}s\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)C_{0}^{m-1}A^{3}C_{0}^{-(m-1)}\epsilon}{(1+v^{b})|y-v^{\alpha}|}\operatorname{min}\{1,e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\}.

By Remark
3.11
we further obtain that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\big[I_{2}(y,v,s)\big]\textup{d}s\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)C_{0}^{m-1}A^{3}C_{0}^{-(m-1)}\epsilon}{(1+v^{b})|y-v^{\alpha}|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\}}.

(3.46)

Remark 3.12
.

Notice that since
C_{0}^{m-1}
is of order
\mathcal{O}(A)
by (
2.9
), this implies in particular that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\big[I_{2}(y,v,s)\big]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)A^{3}\epsilon}{(1+v^{b})|y-v^{\alpha}|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\}}

\displaystyle=\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,b,m)C_{0}^{m-1}A^{2}\epsilon}{(1+v^{b})|y-v^{\alpha}|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq v^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\}}.

The reason we keep the form (
3.46
) is because this estimate is part of the more general estimate in (
3.14
).

We now estimate the region where
\{(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}
. In this region, it holds that
|y-w^{\alpha}|\geq|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|
. Then

\displaystyle\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\epsilon}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|^{m}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}.

We have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{\textup{d}w}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\alpha)v^{1-\alpha}\int_{y-v^{\alpha}}^{y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}}\frac{\textup{d}z}{|z|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{z\leq-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\alpha)v^{1-\alpha}\int_{(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-y}^{v^{\alpha}-y}\frac{\textup{d}z}{z}\mathbbm{1}_{\{z\geq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\alpha)v^{1-\alpha}\bigg[\frac{t}{\epsilon}+|\ln{v^{\alpha}}|\bigg]

(3.47)

since
0\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}
. We can thus conclude that in this region, we have that

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\bigg[\int_{\delta v}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\mathbbm{1}_{\{(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\leq y\leq v^{\alpha}+C_{0}^{-1}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}\bigg]\textup{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,b)A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}[v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}t+v^{\gamma+1}\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{v\geq 1\}}+v^{\gamma-\alpha}\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{v\leq 1\}}]}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{1+|e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha})+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})|^{m}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha})+e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}(v^{\alpha}-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha})\geq C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}\}}\textup{d}s.

We can now conclude as before using (
3.35
).
This concludes this case.
We now bound

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\delta v}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s.

Notice that we only have to make sure that we have sufficiently fast decay in the
v
variable. We then follow the computations for the region
w\in[\delta v,\frac{v}{2}]
in order to conclude. More precisely, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle I_{3}(y,v,s):=

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{\delta v}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{1}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,b)A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})(1+v^{\gamma+1})}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{1+e^{\frac{(m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha})s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}}}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w,

for a sufficiently small
\delta<\frac{1}{2}
.
We have that

\displaystyle I_{3}(y,v,s)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(b)A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma}}{1+v^{b}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{1}{1+w^{b}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{(\frac{v-w}{3})^{\alpha}\leq y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(b)A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma}}{1+v^{b}}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m}}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{(\frac{v-w}{3})^{\alpha}\leq y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w.

We have that
(\frac{v-w}{3})^{\alpha}\leq y
, which implies that for
\delta
sufficiently small it holds that
y-w^{\alpha}\geq(\frac{v-w}{3})^{\alpha}-w^{\alpha}\geq v^{\alpha}\bigg[\big(\frac{1-\delta}{3}\big)^{\alpha}-\delta^{\alpha}\bigg]\geq C(\delta,\alpha)v^{\alpha}
. We thus have that

\displaystyle I_{3}(y,v,s)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C(\delta,\alpha,\gamma,b)A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})(1+v^{\gamma+1})}\int_{0}^{\delta v}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\geq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{1+e^{\frac{(m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha})s}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{(\frac{v-w}{3})^{\alpha}\leq y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w

and we can proceed as in the region
w\in[\delta v,\frac{v}{2}]
since
m>\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}+2
.

ii)

We continue by bounding from above the term

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{3}(y,v-w,s)T_{2}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s.

(3.48)

We will show that

\displaystyle I_{4}(y,v,s):=

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{3}(y,v-w,s)T_{2}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\alpha,\gamma,b)M_{2}A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})(1+v^{\gamma+1})}\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(\frac{v-w}{3})^{\alpha}\}}}{1+e^{\frac{(m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha})s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|^{m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{(\frac{w}{3})^{\alpha}\leq y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-w^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w.

Since
0\leq y\leq(\frac{v-w}{3})^{\alpha}
, we have that
|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|\geq C(\alpha)(v-w)^{\alpha}\geq C(\alpha)v^{\alpha}.
Thus

\displaystyle I_{4}(y,v,s)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2M_{2}A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma}}{1+v^{b}}\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(\frac{v-w}{3})^{\alpha}\}}}{1+e^{\frac{ms}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|^{m}}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{(\frac{w}{3})^{\alpha}\leq y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-w^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C(\alpha,\gamma,b)M_{2}A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})(1+v^{\gamma+1})}\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(\frac{v-w}{3})^{\alpha}\}}}{1+e^{\frac{(m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha})s}{\epsilon}}|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|^{m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{(\frac{w}{3})^{\alpha}\leq y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-w^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w.

We further have that

\displaystyle I_{4}(y,v,s)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C(\alpha,\gamma,b)M_{2}A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})(1+v^{\gamma+1})}\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{(m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha})s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|^{m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-w^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w\mathbbm{1}_{\{0\leq y\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}.

We bound
\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-w^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w
as in (
3.4
). Thus

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-w^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w\leq C(\alpha)v^{1-\alpha}\bigg[\frac{t}{\epsilon}+|\ln{v^{\alpha}}|\bigg].

(3.49)

It follows that

\displaystyle I_{4}(y,v,s)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C(\alpha,\gamma,b)M_{2}A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}\operatorname{max}\{v^{\gamma+1},v^{\gamma-\alpha}\}(\epsilon+t)}{(1+v^{b})(1+v^{\gamma+1})}

\displaystyle\times\frac{e^{\frac{s}{\epsilon}}}{1+e^{\frac{(m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha})s}{\epsilon}}|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|^{m-\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{0\leq y\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}.

We then use (
3.35
) since
m>\frac{\gamma+1}{\alpha}+2
in order to deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\big[I_{4}(y,v,s)\big]\textup{d}s\leq\frac{C(\alpha,\gamma,b)M_{2}A^{3}C_{0}^{m-1}(\epsilon+t)\epsilon}{(1+v^{b})|y-v^{\alpha}|}\operatorname{min}\{1,e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y-v^{\alpha}|\}.

Proof of (
3.15
).
We now bound from above the term

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{2}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s.

(3.50)

We notice that
(\frac{v-w}{3})^{\alpha}\leq y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\leq w^{\alpha}
, which implies that
w\geq\frac{v}{4}
. In other words, it suffices to bound from above the following term

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\int_{\frac{v}{4}}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{2}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w\textup{d}s.

(3.51)

We start by estimating

\displaystyle I_{5}(y,v,s):=

\displaystyle v^{\gamma}\int_{\frac{v}{4}}^{\frac{v}{2}}T_{2}(y,v-w,s)T_{2}(y,w,s)\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq 4A^{4}C_{0}^{2(m-1)}v^{\gamma}\int_{\frac{v}{4}}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{1}{(1+w^{b})(1+(v-w)^{b})}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-w^{\alpha}|}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(b)A^{4}C_{0}^{2(m-1)}v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\int_{\frac{v}{4}}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-w^{\alpha}|}\frac{\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(v-w)^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-(v-w)^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(b)A^{4}C_{0}^{2(m-1)}v^{\gamma}}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\frac{1}{|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|}\int_{\frac{v}{4}}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{\epsilon^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-w^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}.

Following the computations in (
3.4
), it holds that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{v}{2}}\frac{\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}}{|y-w^{\alpha}|}\textup{d}w\leq C(\alpha)v^{1-\alpha}\bigg[\frac{t}{\epsilon}+|\ln{v^{\alpha}}|\bigg].

(3.52)

Thus, we have that

\displaystyle I_{5}(y,v,s)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\alpha,b)A^{4}C_{0}^{2(m-1)}\epsilon[v^{\gamma+1-\alpha}t+v^{\gamma+\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{v\geq 1\}}+v^{\gamma-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\epsilon\mathbbm{1}_{\{v\leq 1\}}]}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\frac{1}{|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\alpha,b)\operatorname{max}\{v^{\gamma+\frac{1}{2}},v^{\gamma-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\}(t+\epsilon)}{(1+v^{b})^{2}}\frac{A^{4}C_{0}^{m-1}\epsilon}{|y-(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}|}\mathbbm{1}_{\{y\leq(\frac{v}{2})^{\alpha}-\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{s}{\epsilon}}\}}

and then we can bound
v^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}S_{\epsilon}(t-s)\big[I_{5}(y,v,s)\big]\textup{d}s
in the same manner as
L(y,v,t),
where
L(y,v,t)
is as in (
3.4
). We then conclude that (
3.15
) holds using estimates similar to (
3.43
) and (
3.4
).

This concludes our proof.
∎

4
Region
y\leq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}

4.1
Moment estimates

In this section we prove that

Proposition 4.1
.

There exists a sufficiently small
T\leq 1
and a sufficiently small
\epsilon_{1}\in(0,1)
, such that for all
\epsilon\leq\epsilon_{1}
and for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
the following statement holds. Let
b\geq\overline{b}(\gamma,\alpha)
,
m\geq\frac{b}{\alpha}+1
in (
2.11
)-(
2.13
), where
\overline{b}(\gamma,\alpha)
is as in (
2.18
). Let
H_{n}
be as in (
2.51
). If

\displaystyle H_{n}(y,v,t)\leq

\displaystyle\textup{ }T_{1}(y,v,t)+T_{2}(y,v,t)+T_{3}(y,v,t),

where
T_{1},T_{2},T_{3}
are as in (
2.11
)-(
2.13
), then it holds that
H_{n+1}(y,v,t)\leq T_{3}(y,v,t),
for all
t\in[0,T]
and
y\leq(\frac{v}{3})^{\alpha}
,
v\in(0,\infty)
.

Remark 4.2
.

T
will be chosen in (
4.42
) and depends on
A
in (
2.1
).

Let

\xi\in C([0,\infty))\,,\quad\xi\colon[0,\infty)\rightarrow[0,1]\;\mbox{ such that }\xi(v)=1\mbox{ if }v\geq 1\mbox{ and }\xi(v)=0\mbox{ if }v\leq\frac{1}{2}.

(4.1)

The idea behind the proof of Proposition
4.1
is to show that
G_{\epsilon}(y,v,t)=e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\tilde{G}_{\epsilon}(y,v,t)
(or a suitably modified version of this function) is a supersolution for (
2.51
), where
\tilde{G}_{\epsilon}
solves

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\tilde{G}_{\epsilon}(y,v,t)+\frac{1}{\epsilon}(v^{\alpha}-y)\partial_{y}\tilde{G}_{\epsilon}(y,v,t)+\frac{Lv^{\gamma}\xi(v)}{1+|y|^{d}}\partial_{v}\tilde{G}_{\epsilon}(y,v,t)

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle(1+v^{b})\tilde{G}_{\epsilon}(y,v,0)

\displaystyle=\frac{A}{1+|y-v^{\alpha}|^{m}},

with
A
as in (
2.1
) and for some suitably chosen
L>0
and
d\in\mathbb{N}
. We will define
d
in (
4.7
) and
L
in (
4.37
) and notice that
L
depends on
A
. We will then choose
\epsilon_{1}
in Proposition
4.1
to depend on
L
. For the choice of
\epsilon_{1}
, we refer to Proposition
4.4
and Proposition
4.7
. Notice that
G_{\epsilon}
satisfies the following equation

\displaystyle\partial_{t}G_{\epsilon}(y,v,t)+\frac{1}{\epsilon}(v^{\alpha}-y)\partial_{y}G_{\epsilon}(y,v,t)+\frac{Lv^{\gamma}\xi(v)}{1+|y|^{d}}\partial_{v}G_{\epsilon}(y,v,t)-\frac{1}{\epsilon}G_{\epsilon}(y,v,t)=0.

(4.2)

We first prove some moment estimates.

Proposition 4.3

(Moment estimates)
.

Let
T\leq 1
,
k\geq 0
. Assume that
P:\mathbb{R}\times(0,\infty)\times[0,T]\rightarrow[0,\infty)
is such that

\displaystyle P(y,v,t)\leq T_{1}(y,v,t)+T_{2}(y,v,t)+T_{3}(y,v,t),

for all
t\in[0,T]
, where
T_{1},T_{2},T_{3}
are as in (
2.11
)-(
2.13
) with
b>k+1
,
m\geq\frac{b}{\alpha}+1
in (
2.11
)-(
2.12
). It then holds that there exists a constant
K_{1}>0
, which depends on
\alpha,\gamma,b,m,
such that

\displaystyle\int_{(0,\infty)}w^{k}P(y,w,t)\textup{d}w\leq\frac{K_{1}A^{3}}{1+|y|^{\frac{b-k-1}{\alpha}}},\textup{ for all }t\in[0,T],y\in\mathbb{R},

(4.3)

where
A
is as in (
2.1
). In particular, for
k=1
, it holds that if
b>2
, then

\displaystyle\int_{(0,\infty)}wP(y,w,t)\textup{d}w\leq\frac{K_{1}A^{3}}{1+|y|^{\frac{b-2}{\alpha}}},\textup{ for all }t\in[0,T],y\in\mathbb{R}.

(4.4)

Proof.

We begin by noticing that

\displaystyle(1+v^{b})[T_{1}(y,v,t)+T_{2}(y,v,t)+T_{3}(y,v,t)]\leq

\displaystyle C(\alpha,\gamma,b,m)A^{3}\big[e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\psi(e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha}))\chi_{1}(y,v,t)

\displaystyle+\frac{\epsilon}{|y-v^{\alpha}|}\chi_{2}(y,v,t)+e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\psi(e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-v^{\alpha}))\chi_{3}(y,v,t)\big],

where
\chi_{1},\chi_{2},\chi_{3}
are as in (
2.14
)-(
2.16
). This is since by the definition of
C_{0}
in (
2.9
), we have that
C_{0}
is of order
A^{\frac{1}{m-1}}
.

Let
y\geq 0
. We distinguish between different regions.

•

Assume first that
\{y\geq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\}
. Then

\displaystyle\int_{\{y\geq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\}}w^{k}P(y,w,t)\textup{d}w\leq\int_{(0,\infty)}\frac{2Aw^{k}e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}}{(1+w^{b})(1+e^{\frac{tm}{\epsilon}}|y-w^{\alpha}|^{m})}\textup{d}w.

We make the change of variables

z=e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}(y-w^{\alpha})
and we obtain that

\displaystyle\int_{\{y\geq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\}}w^{k}P(y,w,t)\textup{d}w

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(-\infty,e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}y)}\frac{2A(y-e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}z)^{\frac{k+1-\alpha}{\alpha}}}{(1+(y-e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}z)^{\frac{b}{\alpha}})(1+|z|^{m})}\textup{d}z.

On one side, if
|z|\leq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}y}{2}
, it holds that
\frac{y}{2}\leq y-e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}z\leq\frac{3y}{2}
. Thus

\displaystyle\int_{|z|\leq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}y}{2}}\frac{(y-e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}z)^{\frac{k+1-\alpha}{\alpha}}}{(1+(y-e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}z)^{\frac{b}{\alpha}})(1+|z|^{m})}\textup{d}z\leq\frac{C(\alpha,b)|y|^{\frac{k+1-\alpha}{\alpha}}}{1+|y|^{\frac{b}{\alpha}}}\int_{(-\infty,\infty)}\frac{C(\alpha,b)}{1+|z|^{m}}\leq\frac{C(\alpha,b)|y|^{\frac{k+1-\alpha}{\alpha}}}{1+|y|^{\frac{b}{\alpha}}}.

(4.5)

On the other hand,
\frac{(y-e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}z)^{\frac{k+1-\alpha}{\alpha}}}{(1+(y-e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}z)^{\frac{b}{\alpha}})}\leq 1
. Thus

\displaystyle\int_{|z|\geq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}y}{2}}\frac{(y-e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}z)^{\frac{k+1-\alpha}{\alpha}}}{(1+(y-e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}z)^{\frac{b}{\alpha}})(1+|z|^{m})}\textup{d}z\leq\int_{|z|\geq\frac{e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}y}{2}}\frac{\textup{d}z}{1+|z|^{m}}\leq\frac{C}{1+(e^{\frac{t}{\epsilon}}|y|)^{m-1}}\leq\frac{C}{1+|y|^{m-1}}.

(4.6)

Combining (
4.5
) with (
4.6
) and the fact that
m\geq\frac{b}{\alpha}+1
, we obtain that (
4.3
) holds in the region
\{y\geq w^{\alpha}-C_{0}^{-1}\epsilon^{-\frac{1}{m-1}}e^{-\frac{t}{\epsilon}}\}
.

•

We now analyze the contribution of the region
